---
title: "Field Dictionary"
description: "957 field definitions, organized by domain and resource."
---

Open a resource to inspect its fields. **Required** describes a valid resource when it exists; it does not guarantee that a provider supplies the value. Examples are illustrative. A blank source example is shown as “Not specified.”

Resource prefixes are documentation context. For example, `bank_transaction.amount.amount` means `data.amount.amount` in a bank transaction record. Common metadata is inherited at the same object level.

## Platform

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="common" id="common" description="16 fields">

#### `common.id`

**F0001** · `id` · Required · Nullable: No

Stable identifier scoped to the client tenant.

- **Format / enum / units:** Opaque AMLOK identifier
- **Origin:** AMLOK managed
- **Classification:** Confidential
- **Availability:** Required when resource exists
- **Normalization / validation:** Never use a provider ID as the public primary key.
- **Source families:** AMLOK
- **Example:** `res_01JAMLOK`

#### `common.object`

**F0002** · `string` · Required · Nullable: No

Resource type corresponding to the Entities tab.

- **Format / enum / units:** UTF-8 string
- **Origin:** AMLOK managed
- **Classification:** Confidential
- **Availability:** Required when resource exists
- **Normalization / validation:** Preserve original evidence; do not invent missing values.
- **Source families:** AMLOK
- **Example:** `bank_account`

#### `common.subject_id`

**F0003** · `id` · Optional · Nullable: Yes

Business or person to which this record relates.

- **Format / enum / units:** Opaque AMLOK identifier
- **Origin:** AMLOK managed
- **Classification:** Confidential
- **Availability:** Source, module, country, consent and contracted permissions determine population.
- **Normalization / validation:** Null for tenant-level configuration and unresolved subjects.
- **Source families:** AMLOK
- **Example:** `biz_01JAMLOK`

#### `common.extensions`

- **Classification:** Restricted
- **Availability:** Source, module, country, consent and contracted permissions determine population.

- **Source families:** AMLOK

#### `reconciliation_link.allocated_amount.amount`

**F0982** · `decimal` · Optional · Nullable: Yes

- **Format / enum / units:** Decimal string; no exponent; no binary float arithmetic
- **Origin:** AMLOK derived
- **Classification:** Confidential
- **Availability:** Source, module, country, consent and contracted permissions determine population.
- **Normalization / validation:** Pair with currency; preserve precision; zero differs from unavailable.
- **Source families:** AMLOK; source-provided linkage
- **Example:** `1234.56`

#### `reconciliation_link.allocated_amount.currency`

#### `workflow_run.product`

**API contract field** · `string` · Required

Insights for full underwriting or its stages; Signal for monitoring and included reassessment.

See [processing lifecycle](/api-reference/processing-lifecycle) and [evidence traceability](/api-reference/evidence-traceability).


#### `workflow_run.input_manifest_id`

**API contract field** · `string` · Required

Immutable evidence manifest used for this run, including exact source record versions.

See [processing lifecycle](/api-reference/processing-lifecycle) and [evidence traceability](/api-reference/evidence-traceability).


#### `workflow_run.initial_input_manifest_id`

**API contract field** · `string` · Required

Initial immutable manifest retained when accepted missing evidence creates a successor for an unfinished run. Audit events identify every replacement; completed outputs remain immutable.

See [processing lifecycle](/api-reference/processing-lifecycle) and [evidence traceability](/api-reference/evidence-traceability).


#### `workflow_run.workload_id`

**API contract field** · `string` · Required

Read-only workload report with observed counts and agreed limits.

See [processing lifecycle](/api-reference/processing-lifecycle) and [evidence traceability](/api-reference/evidence-traceability).


#### `workflow_run.billing_usage_id`

**API contract field** · `nullable` · Required

The one successful product charge associated with the result. Null before charging, or for an included stage with no separate charge.

See [processing lifecycle](/api-reference/processing-lifecycle) and [evidence traceability](/api-reference/evidence-traceability).


#### `workflow_run.monitoring_period_id`

**API contract field** · `nullable` · Required

Shared borrower/business/workspace UTC calendar month. Null for Insights.

See [processing lifecycle](/api-reference/processing-lifecycle) and [evidence traceability](/api-reference/evidence-traceability).


#### `workflow_run.blocked_reason`

**API contract field** · `nullable` · Required

Reason processing cannot continue. Null when unblocked. Inspect the enum and processing lifecycle before retrying.

See [processing lifecycle](/api-reference/processing-lifecycle) and [evidence traceability](/api-reference/evidence-traceability).


#### `workflow_run.stop_reason`

**API contract field** · `nullable` · Required

Why unfinished processing stopped: account cancellation, parent cancellation, or revoked consent. Null when no stop applies.

See [processing lifecycle](/api-reference/processing-lifecycle) and [evidence traceability](/api-reference/evidence-traceability).

</Accordion>
